  "meta_commentary": "Land in dependency order: T1 and T2 are independent and unblock everything; T3 is also independent and small. T6 should be updated alongside T4/T5 and should not be merged without green tests.\n\nThe nested-try retry in T2 is the subtlety that makes FLAG-002 safe: the outer try/except \\u2192 return None contract stays identical, so every caller that branches on a falsey return (e.g. admin_chat_cog.py:703-710, :771-773) keeps its fail-closed recovery path. Do not refactor this into a helper \\u2014 inline is the point.\n\nThe _set_intent_status_message helper should stay ~40 lines. If it's growing past that, you are probably duplicating send logic or over-abstracting; simplify. It is the SINGLE point of creation/edit for the status message within AdminChatCog.\n\nFor the final reply, use message.reply(...) (not channel.send with a reference) so the Discord client renders the reply affordance. mention_author=True ensures the tag actually pings. suppress_embeds=True is the key piece that stops the Solana Explorer link from rendering a card \\u2014 without it the whole collapse is defeated.\n\nStatus wording: default to concise single-line transitions \\u2014 'Verifying wallet with a small test payment\\u2026' \\u2192 '<@recipient> Test payment confirmed.   "meta_commentary": "Land in dependency order: T1 and T2 are independent and unblock everything; T3 is also independent and small. T4 and T5 MUST land together \\u2014 T4 reuses receipt_prompt_message_id to point at the status message, and T5 is the only thing that stops _cleanup_admin_intent_messages from deleting it on admin approval. If you land T4 without T5 you WILL delete the shared status message mid-flow. T6 should be updated alongside T4/T5 and should not be merged without green tests.\n\nThe nested-try retry in T2 is the subtlety that makes FLAG-002 safe: the outer try/except \\u2192 return None contract stays identical, so every caller that branches on a falsey return (e.g. admin_chat_cog.py:703-710, :771-773) keeps its fail-closed recovery path. Do not refactor this into a helper \\u2014 inline is the point.\n\nThe _set_intent_status_message helper should stay ~40 lines. If it's growing past that, you are probably duplicating send logic or over-abstracting; simplify. It is the SINGLE point of creation/edit for the status message within AdminChatCog.\n\nFor the final reply, use message.reply(...) (not channel.send with a reference) so the Discord client renders the reply affordance. mention_author=True ensures the tag actually pings. suppress_embeds=True is the key piece that stops the Solana Explorer link from rendering a card \\u2014 without it the whole collapse is defeated.\n\nStatus wording: default to concise single-line transitions \\u2014 'Verifying wallet with a small test payment\\u2026' \\u2192 '<@recipient> Test payment confirmed. Please reply here with `confirmed` once you see it in your wallet.' \\u2192 'Confirmation received. Awaiting admin approval for the final payout.' \\u2192 'Payout queued for sending.' (from payment_ui_cog on admin approval) \\u2192 'Payout sending\\u2026' \\u2192 reply 'Payment sent \\u2014 X.XXXX SOL. <explorer_link>'. The plan authors flagged these are replaceable \\u2014 if the user asks for different wording during execution, change the strings but not the structure.\n\nFailure branch default: edit the status message to a short terminal failure line rather than going silent. Assumption #5 in the plan metadata. If the user redirects, only the string changes.\n\nBe careful with the test-confirmed transition's reuse of receipt_prompt_message_id. This works only because T5's cleanup exclusion filters status_message_id out of the delete list. If you reorder implementation and ship T4 alone, the cleanup sweep WILL delete the shared status message on admin approval.\n\nDo NOT widen worker-side suppression to other producers. The explicit success criterion requires non-admin_chat producers to keep receiving the generic result card. The negative test (test_handle_payment_result_still_notifies_non_admin_chat_producers) exists to guard this.\n\nFor T7, the throwaway repro script lives under /tmp and is deleted after it runs. Do not commit it, do not add it to tests/. It exists purely to give the executor ground-truth confirmation that the channel collapse works end-to-end at runtime (beyond what unit tests assert).\n\nIf pytest surfaces pre-existing failures unrelated to this change, record them in baseline_test_failures at the START of execution (before making code changes) so the reviewer can distinguish regressions from baseline noise. The plan has no instructions to fix unrelated baseline failures \\u2014 do not attempt to.",

  "meta_commentary": "Land in dependency order: T1 and T2 are independent and unblock everything; T3 is also independent and small. T4 and T5 MUST land together \\u2014 T4 reuses receipt_prompt_message_id to point at the status message, and T5 is the only thing that stops _cleanup_admin_intent_messages from deleting it on admin approval. If you land T4 without T5 you WILL delete the shared status message mid-flow. T6 should be updated alongside T4/T5 and should not be merged without green tests.\n\nThe nested-try retry in T2 is the subtlety that makes FLAG-002 safe: the outer try/except \\u2192 return None contract stays identical, so every caller that branches on a falsey return (e.g. admin_chat_cog.py:703-710, :771-773) keeps its fail-closed recovery path. Do not refactor this into a helper \\u2014 inline is the point.\n\nThe _set_intent_status_message helper should stay ~40 lines. If it's growing past that, you are probably duplicating send logic or over-abstracting; simplify. It is the SINGLE point of creation/edit for the status message within AdminChatCog.\n\nFor the final reply, use message.reply(...) (not channel.send with a reference) so the Discord client renders the reply affordance. mention_author=True ensures the tag actually pings. suppress_embeds=True is the key piece that stops the Solana Explorer link from rendering a card \\u2014 without it the whole collapse is defeated.\n\nStatus wording: default to concise single-line transitions \\u2014 'Verifying wallet with a small test payment\\u2026' \\u2192 '<@recipient> Test payment confirmed. Please reply here with `confirmed` once you see it in your wallet.' \\u2192 'Confirmation received. Awaiting admin approval for the final payout.' \\u2192 'Payout queued for sending.' (from payment_ui_cog on admin approval) \\u2192 'Payout sending\\u2026' \\u2192 reply 'Payment sent \\u2014 X.XXXX SOL. <explorer_link>'.   "meta_commentary": "Land in dependency order: T1 and T2 are independent and unblock everything; T3 is also independent and small. T4 and T5 MUST land together \\u2014 T4 reuses receipt_prompt_message_id to point at the status message, and T5 is the only thing that stops _cleanup_admin_intent_messages from deleting it on admin approval. If you land T4 without T5 you WILL delete the shared status message mid-flow. T6 should be updated alongside T4/T5 and should not be merged without green tests.\n\nThe nested-try retry in T2 is the subtlety that makes FLAG-002 safe: the outer try/except \\u2192 return None contract stays identical, so every caller that branches on a falsey return (e.g. admin_chat_cog.py:703-710, :771-773) keeps its fail-closed recovery path. Do not refactor this into a helper \\u2014 inline is the point.\n\nThe _set_intent_status_message helper should stay ~40 lines. If it's growing past that, you are probably duplicating send logic or over-abstracting; simplify. It is the SINGLE point of creation/edit for the status message within AdminChatCog.\n\nFor the final reply, use message.reply(...) (not channel.send with a reference) so the Discord client renders the reply affordance. mention_author=True ensures the tag actually pings. suppress_embeds=True is the key piece that stops the Solana Explorer link from rendering a card \\u2014 without it the whole collapse is defeated.\n\nStatus wording: default to concise single-line transitions \\u2014 'Verifying wallet with a small test payment\\u2026' \\u2192 '<@recipient> Test payment confirmed. Please reply here with `confirmed` once you see it in your wallet.' \\u2192 'Confirmation received. Awaiting admin approval for the final payout.' \\u2192 'Payout queued for sending.' (from payment_ui_cog on admin approval) \\u2192 'Payout sending\\u2026' \\u2192 reply 'Payment sent \\u2014 X.XXXX SOL. <explorer_link>'. The plan authors flagged these are replaceable \\u2014 if the user asks for different wording during execution, change the strings but not the structure.\n\nFailure branch default: edit the status message to a short terminal failure line rather than going silent. Assumption #5 in the plan metadata. If the user redirects, only the string changes.\n\nBe careful with the test-confirmed transition's reuse of receipt_prompt_message_id. This works only because T5's cleanup exclusion filters status_message_id out of the delete list. If you reorder implementation and ship T4 alone, the cleanup sweep WILL delete the shared status message on admin approval.\n\nDo NOT widen worker-side suppression to other producers. The explicit success criterion requires non-admin_chat producers to keep receiving the generic result card. The negative test (test_handle_payment_result_still_notifies_non_admin_chat_producers) exists to guard this.\n\nFor T7, the throwaway repro script lives under /tmp and is deleted after it runs. Do not commit it, do not add it to tests/. It exists purely to give the executor ground-truth confirmation that the channel collapse works end-to-end at runtime (beyond what unit tests assert).\n\nIf pytest surfaces pre-existing failures unrelated to this change, record them in baseline_test_failures at the START of execution (before making code changes) so the reviewer can distinguish regressions from baseline noise.
